# Master English Frequency Lexicon (Top 5,000 Words)

Structured frequency vocabulary notes based on Corpus of Contemporary American English (COCA) and New General Service List (NGSL).

**Total Vocabulary Count:** 5,000 ranked words across 5 detailed batch files (1,000 words per note) with definitions, phonetics, audio, and Hindi meanings.

- 🎓 **Oxford 3000 / 5000 by CEFR Level:** [../OXFORD 5000/README.md](<../OXFORD 5000/README.md>) — Level-based vocabulary (A1 to C1).
- 📖 **Oxford Complete Word List:** [../OXFORD 5000/VOCABULARY (CEFR Levels).md](<../OXFORD 5000/VOCABULARY (CEFR Levels).md>) — 5,948 words by CEFR.
